# Batmobile animation record (G1-10)

Every `AnimSequence` in the four Batmobile packages, joined to its owning `AnimSet`, the AnimSet's `TrackBoneNames`, the umodel `RemoveTracks` config, the PSA header and the sequence's `Notifies`. Generated by `Tools/scripts/grab_anims.py`; nothing here is hand-listed.

**Verdict:** 253/253 sequences named from SequenceName; 13 sets + 3 AnimTrees; 13 PSA exports (253 sequences, 3147888 keys); 95 with notifies (309 items); 253/253 have bone lists and mechanism classes; 0 length and 0 frame mismatches.

## 1. Totals

| quantity | value |
|---|---|
| sequences | 253 |
| named from `SequenceName` | 253/253 |
| AnimSets | 13 |
| AnimTrees | 3 |
| PSA exports matched | 13 sets / 253 sequences |
| PSA keys read | 3,147,888 |
| active bone tracks | 7380 |
| moved bone tracks (>= 0.5 cm or 0.5 deg) | 4045 |
| sequences with notifies | 95 (309 items) |
| notify classes | 13 |
| `AnimNotify_Sound` / `RAnimNotify_Script` | 204 / 35 |
| names repeated across packages | 51 |
| PSA name differs from SequenceName | 4 |
| length mismatches | 0 |
| frame-count mismatches | 0 |
| PSA exports missing | 0 |

The old `MECHANICS.md` animation table shows `AnimSequence` in the name column because that **is** the export table name for all 253 objects. The human name is the `SequenceName` NameProperty at tag offset 84; this record uses it and keeps the export name in `ANIMATIONS.json` as evidence.

## 3. Mechanism classes

A sequence is assigned one **primary** class from the bones that actually move (>= 0.5 cm or 0.5 deg from frame 0):

* `driver/Batman` - any `Bip01*`, `Gundummy*`, `FcFX_*`, `flower_root` or `ExposeTransform*` track.
* `canopy` - `CockPit_Mesh` / `Batmobile_Nose_Mesh`.
* `fins/air-brakes` - `Aero_Flap*` / `Jet_Fins`.
* `missile bays` - `Weapons_Missiles_*`.
* `cannon` - `Heavy_Cannon*`, `Heavy_Shells`, `Riot_Sup*`.
* `Vulcan` - `Machine_Gun*`.
* `turret` - `Weapons_Turret_*`.
* `wheels/strafe` - `Wheel_*` other than the suspension parents.
* `suspension` - `SUSP*`, `Damper*`, `Wheel_Parent*`, wheel-arch arms.
* `transformation` - `*Shell*`, or three or more strong weapon/body mechanisms moving together. `Weapons_Trans`, `Weapons_Root`, `Weapons_Sides*` and `Weapons_Top_Sides*` are grouped here in the per-sequence mechanism table, but they also move during fire and therefore do not classify a sequence alone.
* `other` - camera, batwing, chair, rear door, winch, jet, sonar, tracker or any motion with no listed mechanism; its bones are listed below.

## 7. Notifies

`time_s` is the float in the sequence's `Notifies` array (seconds; every value is within its sequence length). `AnimNotify_Sound` resolves its `AkEvent`; `RAnimNotify_Script` exposes the called script name; other classes keep their payload.

|---|---|

| `RAnimNotify_Script` | 35 |

| `RAnimNotify_CanCancelAfterHere` | 3 |
| `RAnimNotify_FloorHeight` | 2 |
| `AnimNotify_ClothingMaxDistanceScale` | 1 |

## 8. Names and joins

* `objects.jsonl` records the **class** `AnimSequence` as the object's `name` for all 253 sequences. The human name is the offset-84 `NameProperty` (`SequenceName`).
* `SequenceName` is repeated in the four packages (the same 37 sequence names exist in the base / MK2 / Funhouse / Joker car sets) and some names repeat inside the Joker Camera/Phase2 sets; `ANIMATIONS.json` keeps every instance. 51 names occur more than once.

PSA `ANIMINFO` is sorted by name and truncates `Name[64]` to 63 characters. `grab_anims.py` matches by name + frame count, then prefix + frame count, and records the match kind. The one duplicate inside a set is `Camera`'s `Disarm_Switch_Player`: two package sequences share the name, PSA calls the 301-frame one `Disarm_Switch_Player_180` and the 284-frame one `Disarm_Switch_Player`; frame count disambiguates them. The three long `Phase2_JK_FACE` names are prefix-matched after the 63-character cut, and the two 2007-frame `...Finale` / `...Finale_Ver01` rows truncate to the same 63 characters; they are assigned in full-name order (`Finale` before `Finale_Ver01`).

| where | SequenceName | PSA name | match |
|---|---|---|---|
| Playable_BatmobileJoker_SF / `Camera` | `Disarm_Switch_Player` | `Disarm_Switch_Player_180` | prefix+frames |
| Playable_BatmobileJoker_SF / `Phase2_JK_FACE` | `Main_Ch10_S05_Joker_A1_Warehouse_Two_Face_Gordon_Outro-2Face_Finale_Ver01` | `Main_Ch10_S05_Joker_A1_Warehouse_Two_Face_Gordon_Outro-2Face_Fin` | prefix+frames |
| Playable_BatmobileJoker_SF / `Phase2_JK_FACE` | `Main_Ch10_S05_Joker_A1_Warehouse_Intro_Conversation-Intro_Joker_TiedUp_Joker` | `Main_Ch10_S05_Joker_A1_Warehouse_Intro_Conversation-Intro_Joker_` | prefix+frames |
| Playable_BatmobileJoker_SF / `Phase2_JK_FACE` | `Main_Ch10_S05_Joker_A1_Warehouse_Two_Face_Gordon_Outro-2Face_Finale` | `Main_Ch10_S05_Joker_A1_Warehouse_Two_Face_Gordon_Outro-2Face_Fin` | prefix+frames |

## 9. Key-frames (outside the repository)

`--write` exports one PSA per AnimSet with umodel and writes a compact JSON per set to:

```text
E:/Modding/_BmAKgrab/anims/keys
```

Format: `position_m` is metres in the PSK/UE3 frame (PSA centimetres / 100), `rotation_quat` is `(x, y, z, w)`, `rotation_euler_xyz_rad` is Euler XYZ in radians (`R = Rz * Ry * Rx`). `null` means the set's `RemoveTracks` config says that component was not exported - it is not a zero. `frame_times_s` is `i / PSA rate`; no resampling or root-motion interpretation is applied.

| label | AnimSet | sequences | keyframe file | config |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Batmobile | `BM_Batmobile_Chassis` | 5 | `Playable_Batmobile_SF__BM_Batmobile_Chassis.json` | `E:/Modding/_BmAKgrab/anims/Playable_Batmobile_SF/AnimSet/BM_Batmobile_Chassis.config` |
| Batmobile | `Batmobile_V2_Pose_States` | 32 | `Playable_Batmobile_SF__Batmobile_V2_Pose_States.json` | `E:/Modding/_BmAKgrab/anims/Playable_Batmobile_SF/AnimSet/Batmobile_V2_Pose_States.config` |
| MK2 | `BM_Batmobile_Chassis` | 5 | `Playable_BatmobileMK2_SF__BM_Batmobile_Chassis.json` | `E:/Modding/_BmAKgrab/anims/Playable_BatmobileMK2_SF/AnimSet/BM_Batmobile_Chassis.config` |

The keyframe files are deliberately outside `Research/` (they are large and derived); `--check` compares the two committed records and reports whether the 13 files are present, but does not re-serialize the keyframes.

## 10. Limits

* All 13 AnimSets exported to PSA; no set was skipped. The three AnimTrees are recorded but do not own sequences: `Batmobile_Skel_Ctrl_Door` has no sequence on its `AnimNodeSequence`, and `Batwing_Vehicle_AnimTree` points at `Batwing_Rescue_01`.
* `SequenceLength`, `NumFrames` and `RateScale` are the offset-156/160/168 tags. In this PSA version `ANIMINFO.TrackTime` is the **frame count**, not seconds; `psa_length` in `ANIMATIONS.json` is `TrackTime / PSA rate` and agrees with the package length (0 mismatches). The two values are kept separately.
* The bone list is `TrackBoneNames` minus `all`; `trans` and `rot` components are null in the keyframes. A bone with only one component still counts as animated.
* The `AnimNotify_Sound` with no `AkEvent` reference is recorded with `sound: null`; it is not dropped. Notify payloads outside the two named classes are kept generically, not interpreted.
* `AnimNotify` times are seconds (all 309 are within their sequence's length); the fraction column is only a convenience.
* Mechanism classes are a bone-based taxonomy, not shipped gameplay state. `transformation` groups all three-or-more-mechanism reconfigurations and shell motion; `other` includes camera, batwing, chair, rear door, winch and jet motion.

## 11. Reproduce

```bash
python Tools/scripts/grab_anims.py --write   # ANIMATIONS.json + .md + keyframes
python Tools/scripts/grab_anims.py --check   # re-derive and compare the two records
```

Environment: `BMAK_GAME_DIR` (cooked packages) and `BMAK_ANIM_DIR` (the umodel exports). Defaults are the paths used by the Batmobile lab.
